### getBatchJobsWithChildStats()
#### Timings
  - invocations: 23
  - total duration: PT10.864065876S
  - avg duration: PT0.47235069S
  - min duration: PT0.032223667S
  - max duration: PT0.968312989S

#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= '2025-10-16 08:26:49.247' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
   - timeToFirstResultSet query part timings: invocations: 23 / total duration: PT10.775317076S / avg duration: PT0.468492046S / min duration: PT0.031726969S / max duration: PT0.968241299S
   - prepareTime query part timings: invocations: 23 / total duration: PT0.000078254S / avg duration: PT0.000003402S / min duration: PT0.000001146S / max duration: PT0.000014313S
   - totalTime query part timings: invocations: 23 / total duration: PT10.790583512S / avg duration: PT0.469155804S / min duration: PT0.03217673S / max duration: PT0.968266778S
